Controller → ✅/❌\n5. Service → ✅/❌\n6. 持久化 → ✅/❌\n7. 前端反馈 → ✅/❌\n卡在第 X 步：[说明]\n\n## 八、缺失能力清单\n### P0\n### P1\n### P2\n```\n\n【工作目录】/Users/heal/orion-design\n\n开始执行。先列出要扫描的文件清单，然后逐页深度分析。"},"uuid":"2649446b-34ee-4361-8927-a9b320cac46d","timestamp":"2026-05-22T07:55:56.150Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}

* Smart Deploy API Routes\n3\t *\n4\t * Routes under /api/v1/deploy\n5\t * Handles deployment execution, history, metrics, and rollback\n6\t */\n7\t\n8\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n9\timport { DatabasePool } from '../services/database';\n10\timport { DeployController } from './controllers/DeployController';\n11\timport { SmartDeployService } from '../services/smart-deploy/SmartDeployService';\n12\timport { authenticateUser } from '../middleware/authMiddleware';\n13\timport { requirePermission } from '../middleware/requirePermission';\n14\t\n15\tinterface DeployRoutesOptions {\n16\t  database?: DatabasePool;\n17\t}\n18\t\n19\texport default async function deployRoutes(\n20\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n21\t  options: DeployRoutesOptions\n22\t): Promise<void> {\n23\t  if (!options.database) {\n24\t    console.warn('[DeployRoutes] No database pool provided, routes will not be functional');\n25\t    return;\n26\t  }\n27\t\n28\t  // Initialize service (SmartDeployService creates repositories internally)\n29\t  const smartDeployService = new SmartDeployService(options.database);\n30\t\n31\t  // Initialize controller\n32\t  const controller = new DeployController(smartDeployService);\n33\t\n34\t  // ==================== Deployment Execution ====================\n35\t\n36\t  app.post('/deploy', {\n37\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'write' })]\n38\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n39\t    return controller.deploy(request, reply);\n40\t  });\n41\t\n42\t  // ==================== Deployment Status ====================\n43\t\n44\t  app.get('/deploy/:id', {\n45\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })]\n46\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n47\t    return controller.getStatus(request, reply);\n48\t  });\n49\t\n50\t  // ==================== Deployment History ====================\n51\t\n52\t  app.get('/deploy/history', {\n53\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })]\n54\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n55\t    return controller.getHistory(request, reply);\n56\t  });\n57\t\n58\t  app.get('/deploy/latest/:appName/:environment', {\n59\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })],\n60\t    schema: {\n61\t      params: {\n62\t        type: 'object',\n63\t        required: ['appName', 'environment'],\n64\t        properties: {\n65\t          appName: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 100 },\n66\t          environment: { type: 'string', enum: ['dev', 'staging', 'prod', 'development', 'production', 'pre-prod'] }\n67\t        }\n68\t      }\n69\t    }\n70\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n71\t    return controller.getLatestDeployment(request, reply);\n72\t  });\n73\t\n74\t  // ==================== Deployment Metrics ====================\n75\t\n76\t  app.get('/deploy/metrics', {\n77\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })]\n78\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n79\t    return controller.getMetrics(request, reply);\n80\t  });\n81\t\n82\t  // ==================== Rollback ====================\n83\t\n84\t  app.post('/deploy/:id/rollback', {\n85\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'write' })]\n86\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n87\t    return controller.rollback(request, reply);\n88\t  });\n89\t\n90\t  app.get('/deploy/:id/rollbacks', {\n91\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })]\n92\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n93\t    return controller.getRollbackHistory(request, reply);\n94\t  });\n95\t\n96\t  // ==================== Cancel ====================\n97\t\n98\t  app.post('/deploy/:id/cancel', {\n99\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'write' })]\n100\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. {"parentUuid":"19a5583f-68f6-4def-b08e-d5c071dc80a1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"09338746-ab51-40b7-a30b-3e797eb69648","agentId":"a3176a717c46fee55","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_f0f81f68232241788fc2068a","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Alert Management API Routes\n3\t *\n4\t * 告警关联、去重、抑制功能\n5\t *\n6\t * Prefix: /api/v1/alert\n7\t *\n8\t * Migrated to replyHelper (2026-05-21)\n9\t */\n10\t\n11\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n12\timport { AlertCorrelationService } from '../services/alert/AlertCorrelationService';\n13\timport { AlertDeduplication } from '../services/alert/AlertDeduplication';\n14\timport { AlertSuppressionService } from '../services/alert/AlertSuppressionService';\n15\timport { AlertStatus, Alert, AlertSeverity } from '../services/alert/AlertTypes';\n16\timport { success, created, badRequest, notFound, internalError } from '../utils/replyHelper';\n17\timport { ErrorCodes } from '../types/error-codes';\n18\t\n19\texport default async function alertRoutes(app: FastifyInstance): Promise<void> {\n20\t  // Initialize services\n21\t  const correlationService = new AlertCorrelationService();\n22\t  const deduplication = new AlertDeduplication();\n23\t  const suppressionService = new AlertSuppressionService();\n24\t\n25\t  // Start deduplication service\n26\t  deduplication.start();\n27\t\n28\t  // ==================== Alert Ingestion ====================\n29\t  // POST /alert/ingest - 接收告警\n30\t  app.post('/ingest', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n31\t    const body = request.body as Record<string, unknown>;\n32\t    try {\n33\t      const alert: Alert = {\n34\t        id: `alert-${Date.now()}`,\n35\t        fingerprint: '',\n36\t        name: body.name as string,\n37\t        severity: body.severity as AlertSeverity,\n38\t        status: AlertStatus.FIRING,\n39\t        sourceType: body.sourceType as any,\n40\t        sourceId: body.sourceId as string,\n41\t        sourceName: body.sourceName as string,\n42\t        labels: (body.labels as Record<string, string>) || {},\n43\t        annotations: (body.annotations as Record<string, string>) || {},\n44\t        value: (body.value as number) || 0,\n45\t        threshold: (body.threshold as number) || 0,\n46\t        tenantId: (body.tenantId as string) || 'default',\n47\t        startsAt: new Date(),\n48\t        createdAt: new Date(),\n49\t        updatedAt: new Date(),\n50\t      };\n51\t\n52\t      // Generate fingerprint\n53\t      const fingerprint = deduplication.generateFingerprint(alert);\n54\t      alert.fingerprint = fingerprint.fingerprint;\n55\t\n56\t      // Check suppression\n57\t      const suppressionResult = await suppressionService.processAlert(alert);\n58\t      if (suppressionResult.suppressed) {\n59\t        return success(reply, request, {\n60\t          status: 'suppressed',\n61\t          reason: suppressionResult.reason,\n62\t          alert,\n63\t        });\n64\t      }\n65\t\n66\t      // Process deduplication\n67\t      const processResult = deduplication.processAlert(alert);\n68\t      return created(reply, request, {\n69\t        status: processResult.action === 'create' ? 'created' : 'updated',\n70\t        alert,\n71\t        isDuplicate: processResult.isDuplicate,\n72\t      });\n73\t    } catch (error) {\n74\t      return internalError(reply, request, error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Alert ingest failed');\n75\t    }\n76\t  });\n77\t\n78\t  // ==================== Alert Correlation ====================\n79\t  // POST /alert/correlate - 告警关联分析\n80\t  app.post('/correlate', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n81\t    const body = request.body as Record<string, unknown>;\n82\t    try {\n83\t      const alerts = body.alerts as Alert[];\n84\t      if (!alerts || alerts.length === 0) {\n85\t        return badRequest(reply, request, ErrorCodes.CLIENT_PARAM_INVALID, 'alerts is required');\n86\t      }\n87\t\n88\t      // Update topology health\n89\t      correlationService.updateNodeHealth(alerts);\n90\t\n91\t      // Perform correlation analysis\n92\t      const analysis = correlationService.analyzeRootCause(alerts);\n93\t      return success(reply, request, { analysis });\n94\t    } catch (error) {\n95\t      return internalError(reply, request, error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Correlation analysis failed');\n96\t    }\n97\t  });\n98\t\n99\t  // GET /alert/topology - 获取告警拓扑图\n100\t  app.get('/topology', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"uuid":"f13b517b-bf5c-43bc-a3f4-9f35d89503ff","timestamp":"2026-05-22T07:56:44.015Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"19a5583f-68f6-4def-b08e-d5c071dc80a1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}

* 诊断 Agent API 路由\n3\t *\n4\t * 前缀: /api/v1/diagnostic\n5\t *\n6\t * Migration: Now uses PostgreSQL Repository pattern when database pool is provided.\n7\t * Falls back to in-memory storage for backward compatibility.\n8\t */\n9\t\n10\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n11\timport { DatabasePool } from '../services/database';\n12\timport { authenticateUser } from '../middleware/authMiddleware';\n13\timport { requirePermission } from '../middleware/requirePermission';\n14\timport { DiagnosticRepository } from '../services/diagnostic/DiagnosticRepository';\n15\timport {\n16\t  DiagnosticAgentService,\n17\t  DiagnosticAgentServiceConfig,\n18\t  TriggerDiagnosticRequest,\n19\t  AddSymptomRequest,\n20\t  AddPatternRequest,\n21\t  DiagnosticTriggerType,\n22\t  DiagnosticSessionStatus,\n23\t  DiagnosticCategory,\n24\t} from '../services/diagnostic';\n25\t\n26\texport interface DiagnosticRoutesOptions {\n27\t  diagnosticAgentService?: DiagnosticAgentService;\n28\t  database?: DatabasePool;\n29\t}\n30\t\n31\texport default async function diagnosticRoutes(\n32\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n33\t  options: DiagnosticRoutesOptions\n34\t): Promise<void> {\n35\t  // Initialize service: prefer PostgreSQL-backed service if database is available\n36\t  let service: DiagnosticAgentService;\n37\t  if (options.database) {\n38\t    const repository = new DiagnosticRepository(options.database);\n39\t    const config: DiagnosticAgentServiceConfig = {\n40\t      repository,\n41\t    };\n42\t    service = new DiagnosticAgentService(config);\n43\t  } else if (options.diagnosticAgentService) {\n44\t    service = options.diagnosticAgentService;\n45\t  } else {\n46\t    // Fallback: in-memory only (for tests / no DB)\n47\t    service = new DiagnosticAgentService();\n48\t  }\n49\t\n50\t  // ==================== 诊断触发 ====================\n51\t\n52\t  /**\n53\t   * POST /api/v1/diagnostic/trigger\n54\t   * 触发诊断\n55\t   */\n56\t  app.post(\n57\t    '/trigger',\n58\t    {\n59\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'diagnostic', action: 'execute' })],\n60\t    },\n61\t    async (\n62\t      request: FastifyRequest,\n63\t      reply: FastifyReply\n64\t    ) => {\n65\t      const body = request.body as TriggerDiagnosticRequest;\n66\t      const { triggerType, triggerId, symptoms, tenantId } = body;\n67\t\n68\t      if (!triggerType || !triggerId || !symptoms || symptoms.length === 0) {\n69\t        return reply.status(400).send({\n70\t          error: 'BAD_REQUEST',\n71\t          message: 'triggerType, triggerId, and at least one symptom are required',\n72\t        });\n73\t      }\n74\t\n75\t      const result = await service.triggerDiagnostic({\n76\t        triggerType,\n77\t        triggerId,\n78\t        symptoms,\n79\t        tenantId,\n80\t      });\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. color: 'default', label: '测试环境' },\n58\t};\n59\t\n60\t// Strategy display labels\n61\tconst strategyLabels: Record<string, string> = {\n62\t  rolling: '滚动更新 (Rolling)',\n63\t  'blue-green': '蓝绿部署 (Blue-Green)',\n64\t  canary: '金丝雀发布 (Canary)',\n65\t  recreate: '重建部署 (Recreate)',\n66\t};\n67\t\n68\t// Health check status icon\n69\tconst healthCheckIcon: Record<string, React.ReactNode> = {\n70\t style={{ color: colors.warning[500] }} />,\n73\t  unknown: <InfoCircleOutlined style={{ color: colors.neutral[400] }} />,\n74\t};\n75\t\n76\tconst DeploymentDetail: React.FC = () => {\n77\t  const navigate = useNavigate();\n78\t  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();\n79\t  const [deployment, setDeployment] = useState<Deployment | null>(null);\n80\t  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n81\t  const [isRollingBack, setIsRollingBack] = useState(false);\n82\t  const [rollbackModalVisible, setRollbackModalVisible] = useState(false);\n83\t\n84\t  // Load deployment from API\n85\t  const loadDeployment = async () => {\n86\t    if (!id) return;\n87\t    setLoading(true);\n88\t    try {\n89\t      const response = await getDeployment(id);\n90\t      const data = response.data.data || response.data;\n91\t      setDeployment(data as Deployment);\n92\t    } catch (error: unknown) {\n93\t      if (error instanceof Error) {\n94\t        message.error(`加载部署详情失败：${error.message}`);\n95\t      } else {\n96\t        message.error('加载部署详情失败');\n97\t      }\n98\t    } finally {\n99\t      setLoading(false);\n100\t    }\n101\t  };\n102\t\n103\t  useEffect(() => {\n104\t    loadDeployment();\n105\t  }, [id]);\n106\t\n107\t  // Handle rollback\n108\t  const handleRollback = async () => {\n109\t    if (!deployment) return;\n110\t    setIsRollingBack(true);\n111\t    setRollbackModalVisible(false);\n112\t    try {\n113\t      await rollbackDeployment(deployment.id);\n114\t      message.success('回滚操作已触发，正在执行中...');\n115\t      // Reload to get updated status\n116\t      await loadDeployment();\n117\t    } catch (error: unknown) {\n118\t      if (error instanceof Error) {\n119\t        message.error(`回滚操作失败：${error.message}`);\n120\t      } else {\n121\t        message.error('回滚操作失败');\n122\t      }\n123\t    } finally {\n124\t      setIsRollingBack(false);\n125\t    }\n126\t  };\n127\t\n128\t  if (loading && !deployment) {\n129\t    return (\n130\t      <div style={{ padding: 48, textAlign: 'center' }}>\n131\t        <Spin size=\"large\" />\n132\t      </div>\n133\t    );\n134\t  }\n135\t\n136\t  if (!deployment) {\n137\t    return (\n138\t      <Result\n139\t        status=\"404\"\n140\t        title=\"部署不存在\"\n141\t        subTitle=\"未找到该部署记录\"\n142\t        extra={\n143\t          <Button type=\"primary\" onClick={() => navigate('/deployments')}>\n144\t            返回列表\n145\t          </Button>\n146\t        }\n147\t      />\n148\t    );\n149\t  }\n150\t\n151\t  const env = envConfig[deployment.environment] || {\n152\t    color: 'default',\n153\t    label: deployment.environment,\n154\t  };\n155\t\n156\t  const formatDuration = (seconds?: number) => {\n157\t    if (!seconds) return '-';\n158\t    const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n159\t    const secs = seconds % 60;\n160\t    return minutes > 0 ? `${minutes}m ${secs}s` : `${secs}s`;\n161\t  };\n162\t\n163\t  const stageStatusColor: Record<string, string> = {\n164\t    success: colors.success[500],\n165\t    running: colors.primary[500],\n166\t    failed: colors.error[500],\n167\t    pending: colors.neutral[300],\n168\t  };\n169\t\n170\t  const canRollback = deployment.status === 'success';\n171\t\n172\t  return (\n173\t    <div style={{ padding: 0 }}>\n174\t      {/* Back button and page title */}\n175\t      <div\n176\t        style={{\n177\t          display: 'flex',\n178\t          alignItems: 'center',\n179\t          gap: 16,\n180\t          marginBottom: 24,\n181\t        }}\n182\t      >\n183\t        <Button type=\"text\" icon={<ArrowLeftOutlined />} onClick={() => navigate('/deployments')}>\n184\t          返回列表\n185\t        </Button>\n186\t        <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>\n187\t          <Title level={2} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>\n188\t            <RocketOutlined style={{ marginRight: spacing[2], color: colors.primary[500] }} />\n189\t            部署详情: {deployment.appName}\n190\t          </Title>\n191\t          <Text type=\"secondary\">\n192\t            版本 {deployment.version} · 部署于 {env.label}\n193\t          </Text>\n194\t        </div>\n195\t        <Space>\n196\t          <StatusBadge status={deployment.status as any} size=\"medium\" />\n197\t          {canRollback && (\n198\t            <Button\n199\t              danger\n200\t              icon={<RollbackOutlined />}\n201\t              onClick={() => setRollbackModalVisible(true)}\n202\t              loading={isRollingBack}\n203\t            >\n204\t              回滚到此版本\n205\t            </Button>\n206\t          )}\n207\t        </Space>\n208\t      </div>\n209\t\n210\t      {/* Rollback confirmation modal */}\n211\t      <Modal\n212\t        title=\"确认回滚\"\n213\t        open={rollbackModalVisible}\n214\t        onOk={handleRollback}\n215\t        onCancel={() => setRollbackModalVisible(false)}\n216\t        okText=\"确认回滚\"\n217\t        cancelText=\"取消\"\n218\t        okButtonProps={{ danger: true, loading: isRollingBack }}\n219\t      >\n220\t        <Result\n221\t          status=\"warning\"\n222\t          title={`确定要回滚 ${deployment.appName} 到 ${deployment.version} 吗？`}\n223\t          subTitle=\"回滚操作将恢复此版本的部署，当前版本将被替换。\"\n224\t          icon={<QuestionCircleOutlined />}\n225\t        />\n226\t      </Modal>\n227\t\n228\t      {/* Deployment info card */}\n229\t      <CardPanel>\n230\t        <Descriptions column={4} size=\"small\" <Descriptions.Item label=\"部署版本\">\n235\t            <Tag color=\"purple\">{deployment.version}</Tag>\n236\t          </Descriptions.Item>\n237\t          <Descriptions.Item label=\"目标环境\">\n238\t </Descriptions.Item>\n246\t          <Descriptions.Item label=\"开始时间\">\n247\t            {deployment.startTime ? dayjs(deployment.startTime).format('YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss') : '-'}\n248\t (\n256\t            <Descriptions.Item label=\"提交 Hash\">\n257\t              <Tag color=\"default\">{deployment.commit}</Tag>\n258\t            </Descriptions.Item>\n259\t          )}\n260\t          {deployment.pipelineRunId && (\n261\t            <Descriptions.Item label=\"关联 Pipeline\">\n262\t              <Button\n263\t                type=\"link\"\n264\t                size=\"small\"\n265\t {deployment.rollbackFrom}\n279\t              </Button>\n280\t            </Descriptions.Item>\n281\t          )}\n282\t        </Descriptions>\n283\t      </CardPanel>\n284\t\n285\t      {/* Stage {deployment.stages && deployment.stages.length > 0 ? (\n291\t              <Space direction=\"vertical\" style={{ width: '100%' }} size={12}>\n292\t                {deployment.stages.map((stage, index) => (\n293\t                  <Card\n294\t                    key={stage.id || stage.name}\n295\t                    size=\"small\"\n296\t                    style={{\n297\t                      borderLeft: `4px solid ${stageStatusColor[stage.status] || <Space>\n308\t                        <Text strong style={{ fontSize: spacing[4] }}>\n309\t                          {index + 1}. {stage.name}\n310\t                        </Text>\n311\t                      </Space>\n312\t                      <Space>\n313\t                        {stage.details && (\n314\t                          <Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: spacing[3] }}>\n315\t (\n325\t              <Text type=\"secondary\">暂无阶段数据</Text>\n326\t            )}\n327\t          </CardPanel>\n328\t        </Col>\n329\t\n330\t        {/* Health check status */}\n331\t        <Col {"parentUuid":"443b0975-7e2e-4d66-84c2-4abd9fd1700f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"09338746-ab51-40b7-a30b-3e797eb69648","agentId":"a3176a717c46fee55","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_cd7ff4c4d9e04cfaa4f1de16","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Artifact Registry API Routes\n3\t * 制品仓库 API 路由\n4\t */\n5\t\n6\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n7\timport { DatabasePool } from '../services/database';\n8\timport { ArtifactController } from './controllers/artifact/ArtifactController';\n9\timport { ArtifactRegistryServiceImpl } from '../services/artifact/ArtifactRegistryService';\n10\timport { PostgresArtifactRepository } from '../repositories/ArtifactRepository';\n11\timport { LocalArtifactStorage } from '../storage/ArtifactStorage';\n12\timport { PromotionService, PromotionStage } from '../services/artifact/PromotionService';\n13\t\n14\tinterface ArtifactRoutesOptions {\n15\t  database?: DatabasePool;\n16\t}\n17\t\n18\texport default async function artifactRoutes(\n19\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n20\t  options: ArtifactRoutesOptions\n21\t): Promise<void> {\n22\t  // 初始化依赖\n23\t  const artifactRepository = options.database\n24\t    ? new PostgresArtifactRepository(options.database)\n25\t    : null;\n26\t  const artifactStorage = new LocalArtifactStorage('/tmp/artifacts');\n27\t  const artifactService = artifactRepository\n28\t    ? new ArtifactRegistryServiceImpl(artifactRepository, artifactStorage)\n29\t    : null;\n30\t  const artifactController = artifactService ? new ArtifactController(artifactService) : null;\n31\t  const promotionService = new PromotionService();\n32\t\n33\t  // DB 不可用时的统一错误响应\n34\t  const dbUnavailable = async (_request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n35\t    return reply.status(503).send({\n36\t      error: 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE',\n37\t      message: 'Artifact management requires database connection',\n38\t    });\n39\t  };\n40\t\n41\t  // ==================== 制品管理 ====================\n42\t\n43\t  // POST /artifacts - 创建制品\n44\t  app.post('/artifacts', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n45\t    if (!artifactController) return dbUnavailable(request, reply);\n46\t    return artifactController.create(request, reply);\n47\t  });\n48\t\n49\t  // GET /artifacts - 获取制品列表\n50\t  app.get('/artifacts', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n51\t    if (!artifactController) return dbUnavailable(request, reply);\n52\t    return artifactController.list(request, reply);\n53\t  });\n54\t\n55\t  // GET /artifacts/:id - 获取制品详情\n56\t  app.get('/artifacts/:id', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n57\t    if (!artifactController) return dbUnavailable(request, reply);\n58\t    return artifactController.getById(request, reply);\n59\t  });\n60\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"uuid":"eb22ff17-d8d3-445e-bf87-623f7b6c83aa","timestamp":"2026-05-22T07:58:29.950Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"443b0975-7e2e-4d66-84c2-4abd9fd1700f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}

| 缺失项 | 影响范围 | 证据 |\n|---|--------|---------|------|\n| 1 | **Monitoring 全部后端路由缺失** | `/observability/monitoring/*` 全部 6 个子页面（Dashboard, Metrics, Alerts, Rules, Channels）无法工作 | `routes.ts` 无 `monitoring-routes` import/注册；`monitoring-routes.ts` 文件不存在；仅 `routes.ts.bak` 有历史记录 |\n| 2 | **Observability 全部后端路由缺失** | `/console/observability/*` 全部功能（告警规则、RCA、静默规则、服务健康）无法工作 | `observability-routes.ts` 不存在；`routes.ts:577` 注释\"migrated to monitor-svc\"但 monitor-svc 未部署 |\n| 3 | **DeploymentList 缺少创建部署入口** | 无法从 UI 发起新部署 | `DeploymentList/index.tsx:280-283` 只有\"刷新\"按钮，无\"创建部署\" |\n| 4 | **`/delivery` 模块入口重定向到 `/pipelines`** | 用户访问 `/delivery` 被重定向到 `/pipelines`，交付模块作为概念不存在 | `routes.ts:1423-1425` |\n| 5 | **`/observability` 模块入口重定向到旧路径** | 重定向到 `/console/monitoring`，而该路径又重定向到 `/observability/monitoring`，最终落入 P0-1 | `routes.ts:1428-1431`, `routes.ts:897-900` |\n\n### P1 (严重缺陷 — 核心流程不完整)\n\n| # | 缺失项 | 影响范围 | 证据 |\n|---|--------|---------|------|\n| 1 | PipelineList 无删除按钮 | `deletePipeline()` API 和后端路由存在，但前端无 UI 暴露 | `api/pipelines.ts:127` 定义，但 `PipelineList/index.tsx` 无删除列 |\n| 2 | 所有列表页无 Empty 状态引导 | PipelineList, DeploymentList, Artifacts, Monitoring/*, SelfHealing/*, Diagnostic/* 均无 | 应使用 `<Empty description=\"暂无数据\" />` + 引导操作按钮 |\n| 3 | Artifacts 删除/废弃操作无二次确认 | 直接执行不可逆操作 | `Artifacts/index.tsx:231-243` `handleDelete` 无 Modal.confirm |\n| 4 | DeploymentList 回滚无确认 | `DeploymentList/index.tsx:248-252` 回滚按钮无 onClick handler，无确认 |\n| 5 | Self-Healing IncidentList 无编辑/删除 | 缺少 incident update 和 delete 功能 | `IncidentList.tsx` 仅有列表和创建 |\n| 6 | 颜色硬编码违反 Design Token 规范 | PipelineList, DeploymentList, PipelineRunList 等多处使用 `'green'`/`'red'` 等字符串而非 `colors.*` token | 详见第三章 |\n| 7 | PipelineRunList 轮询逻辑未清理 | `pollingTimerRef` 定义但未在 useEffect cleanup 中清除 | `PipelineRunList/index.tsx:73` 定义，需确认 cleanup 逻辑 |\n\n### P2 (一般缺陷 — 体验优化)\n\n| # | 缺失项 | 影响范围 | 证据 |\n|---|--------|---------|------|\n| 1 | 所有页面无导出功能 | 无 CSV/Excel/PDF 导出 | 所有列表页 |\n| 2 | 所有页面无报表功能 | 无可视化报表 | 所有列表页 |\n| 3 | 页面无权限控制 | 所有页面缺少权限检查 | `routes.ts` 部分路由有 `requiredPermission` 但不完整 |\n| 4 | ObservabilityPage 使用 setTimeout 模拟加载 | `ObservabilityPage.tsx:754` `setTimeout(() => setLoading(false), 500)` 是 Mock |\n| 5 | PipelineEditor 阶段类型使用 emoji 图标 | `PipelineEditor/index.tsx:80-88` 使用 `'🔨'`、`'🧪'` 等 emoji，不专业 |\n| 6 | Monitoring/Dashboard 启动/停止无确认 | 影响线上监控的启停操作无确认弹窗 | `Monitoring/Dashboard.tsx:63-95` |\n| 7 | Diagnostic Sessions 无删除功能 | `Sessions.tsx` 缺少清理历史会话的能力 |"}],"model":"qwen3.6-plus","stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":0,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"type":"assistant","uuid":"5da68147-bb31-4dd5-88aa-e3b4f66e756b","timestamp":"2026-05-22T08:00:22.323Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}
